CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `ChannelEntity` (`serverId` TEXT NOT NULL, `channelUrl` TEXT NOT NULL, `hasAdultContent` INTEGER NOT NULL, `subscriptionKind` TEXT NOT NULL, `isOwner` INTEGER NOT NULL, `hasSubscriptionLevels` INTEGER NOT NULL, `subscribers` INTEGER NOT NULL, `time` INTEGER NOT NULL, `coverUrlChannel` TEXT NOT NULL, `isBlackListed` INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, `channelCoverType` TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '', `channelCoverImageUrl` TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '', `notifySubscription` INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, PRIMARY KEY(`serverId`))
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `StreamEntity` (`serverId` TEXT NOT NULL, `time` INTEGER NOT NULL, `isOnline` INTEGER NOT NULL, `title` TEXT NOT NULL, `titleData` TEXT, `viewersCount` INTEGER NOT NULL, `likesCount` INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, `isLiked` INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, `inPreviewMode` INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, `previewUrl` TEXT NOT NULL, `hostForStreamer` TEXT DEFAULT '', `displayNameHost` TEXT DEFAULT '', `categoryId` TEXT, `hasChatPinnedMessage` INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, `isHidden` INTEGER DEFAULT 0, `viewAllowed` INTEGER NOT NULL, `subscriptionLevelId` TEXT, `subscriptionLevelName` TEXT, `subscriptionLevelPriority` INTEGER, `subscriptionLevelIsArchived` INTEGER, `subscriptionLevelExternalId` TEXT, `createdAt` INTEGER NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY(`serverId`), FOREIGN KEY(`categoryId`) REFERENCES `CategoryEntity`(`serverId`) ON UPDATE NO ACTION ON DELETE SET NULL )
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `_new_HistoryChannelEntity` (`id` TEXT NOT NULL, `channelUrl` TEXT NOT NULL, `hasAdultContent` INTEGER NOT NULL, `author` TEXT NOT NULL, `stream` TEXT, `subscriptionKind` TEXT NOT NULL, `isOwner` INTEGER NOT NULL, `hasSubscriptionLevels` INTEGER NOT NULL, `subscribers` INTEGER NOT NULL, `time` INTEGER NOT NULL, `coverUrlChannel` TEXT NOT NULL, `isBlackListed` INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, `channelCoverType` TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '', `channelCoverImageUrl` TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '', `notifySubscription` INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, `order` INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, PRIMARY KEY(`id`))
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `_new_StreamEntity` (`serverId` TEXT NOT NULL, `time` INTEGER NOT NULL, `isOnline` INTEGER NOT NULL, `title` TEXT NOT NULL, `titleData` TEXT, `viewersCount` INTEGER NOT NULL, `likesCount` INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, `isLiked` INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, `previewUrl` TEXT NOT NULL, `hostForStreamer` TEXT DEFAULT '', `displayNameHost` TEXT DEFAULT '', `categoryId` TEXT, `hasChatPinnedMessage` INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0, `isHidden` INTEGER DEFAULT 0, `viewAllowed` INTEGER NOT NULL, `subscriptionLevelId` TEXT, `subscriptionLevelName` TEXT, `subscriptionLevelPriority` INTEGER, `subscriptionLevelIsArchived` INTEGER, `subscriptionLevelExternalId` TEXT, `createdAt` INTEGER NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY(`serverId`), FOREIGN KEY(`categoryId`) REFERENCES `CategoryEntity`(`serverId`) ON UPDATE NO ACTION ON DELETE SET NULL )
